--- seg-c4b035dfc31cc9b780375633 [needs-review] ---
Another case documented in the National Archive is the "Two Bridges Case." According to the National Archive, in August 1962, an event occurred that is considered one of the most intriguing cases of contact with unidentified objects in Brazil. Known as the "Two Bridges Case," the incident allegedly involved the contact and abduction of a miner in the district of Two Bridges, in the municipality of Diamantina, Minas Gerais. On the 19th and 20th days, a father and son witnessed two objects inside and near their residence. After the contact with the objects, the father disappeared, causing a great commotion in the area and requiring the intervention of the local police. The case also attracted the attention of the Civil Investigation Center for Unidentified Aerial Objects - CICOANI, which sent investigators to inquire about the incident. The boy reported the event to police, doctors, judges, and journalists. According to the evaluation of CICOANI investigators, the boy's account could not be the product of invention, "delirium" or "hallucination," as it was "sophisticated and inconsistent with the sociocultural context."

Another case archived in the National Archive was called Extraterrestrial Reports – UFOs in Santo Antonio de Tauá (PA). According to National Archive information, illustrations were made based on a report of an observation of an unidentified flying object (UFO) that occurred on September 2, 1977, in the municipality of Santo Antonio do Tauá (PA). According to data collected by the National Archive, starting in 1968, various reports of appearances of unidentified flying objects (UFOs) began to be published in the Brazilian press, causing great stir and generating numerous speculations. Popularly known as

===== PAGE 56 [page-dbdcfa200d706cd2e611773f] =====
--- seg-735b4f8247728ea173ab6976 [needs-review] ---
Registration Process for intangible property: "The E.T. Case and the Varginha Identity"

Municipality of Varginha | MG

Year 2023 | Exercise 2025
--- seg-7645b39932d854218f73525e [needs-review] ---
"Flying saucers," the Brazilian authorities referred to these objects as "Unidentified Aerial Objects" (UANIs). According to the National Archive, due to the popularity of the subject, the Brazilian government established the System for the Investigation of Unidentified Aerial Objects (SIOANI), under the IV Air Zone of the Brazilian Air Force – FAB.

Critics of what they termed as the "sensationalism" of the press and denouncing "impostors" and "adventurers," SIOANI agents investigated the phenomenon using a scientific approach. They collected statements from witnesses and, in some cases, conducted inquiries at observation locations. One of the most important stages of the investigation was the contact with the observer. At this stage, in addition to the statement, a psychiatric evaluation of the witness was conducted, and their social and professional profile was drawn. Furthermore, an illustration of the observed phenomenon was made according to the statement – a spoken description of what had been sighted.

The following is a document from the National Archive's collection. The document contains two observation records. It is noted that in one of them, the observer claimed to have seen "crews," meaning extraterrestrial beings.

===== PAGE 60 [page-76dd63696abf3e02a0fd11a3] =====
--- seg-0a0d67d676669b79061a3174 [needs-review] ---
Registration Process for intangible property: "The E.T. Case and the Varginha Identity"

Municipality of Varginha | MG

Year 2023 | Exercise 2025
--- seg-b605692e9edc96bf82359ccc [needs-review] ---
The sighting photographs were taken by the Bahia photographer Almiro Baraúna
(1916-2000). The photographer was invited by the Brazilian Navy to participate in oceanographic research on Trindade Island, off the Espírito Santo coast, to take underwater photographs. However, that day, on board the Admiral Saldanha ship, he captured four images of what would be a spacecraft flying over the island. The negatives of the images were developed in an improvised laboratory on the ship-school and at the time, it was not possible to see the object with precision.

A few days after the landing, photographer Baraúna presented the developed and enlarged photographs to the press, which included images of the unidentified object. This is how the case gained international resonance.

In 1967, nine years after that incident, in a letter dated January 30, 1967, the photographer described to Colonel Colman von Keviczy, director of the Intercontinental UFO Network (ICUFON) in New York, how the sighting had occurred.
--- seg-55c93e7960c4a587e218fad9 [needs-review] ---
On January 16, 1958, the training ship Admiral Saldanha of the Brazilian Navy was anchored in the cove of Trindade Island, approximately 800 miles from the coast of Espírito Santo. It was around 11 a.m., the day was clear, and the crew was preparing to return to Rio de Janeiro when, suddenly, a group of people at the ship's rear, including Reserve Air Force Captain José Viegas, sounded the alarm. In an instant, everyone on the ship's deck, around fifty people, turned to observe a strange, plate-shaped, silver object moving from the sea towards the island. The object made no sound, was luminous, and moved erratically, rising and falling, gently waving, and leaving a trail of white, phosphorescent light behind as it accelerated, which quickly dissipated. As it moved, the object disappeared behind Desejado Peak, and when everyone expected it to reappear on the opposite side, it returned in the same direction, paused for a few seconds, and then shot off at incredible speed, disappearing into the horizon. During its appearance, I managed to take six photos, two of which, due to the chaos on deck, were not usable, while the other four show, in reasonable sequence, the object on the horizon, approaching the island, parked next to the peak (the best one), and finally disappearing in the distance. The film was developed about twenty minutes later, at the captain's request, who wanted to know if the photos were good. The negatives were viewed by almost the entire crew, and everyone agreed to identify the prints later at the Navy's Secret Service. It should be noted that the group of civilians was on board at the Navy's invitation to conduct underwater research and take photographs of the island's underwater fauna. The group consisted of the following individuals: Chief Amilar Vieira Filho, a banker, diver, and athlete; Deputy Chief: Retired Air Force Captain José Viegas; Divers: Aluizio and Mauro; Photographer: Almiro Baraúna.

All components were part, at the time of the events, of the Submarine Hunting Group of Icaraí.

Of the five, only Mauro and Aluizio did not see the object, as they were in the ship's mess hall, and when they came up, attracted by the commotion, the object had already disappeared.

According to rumors heard on board, the ship's electrical equipment stopped working during the object's appearance; what I can confirm is that immediately after setting sail, the ship stopped three times, and the officers provided the most strange versions about the stops. When the ship stopped, the lights dimmed gradually until they went out completely. At that time, several officers went to the deck with binoculars, but the sky was already covered in clouds, and nothing could be observed.

I should add that if it weren't for the indiscretion of a reporter from the "Correio da Manhã" newspaper, who took the photographic copies offered to then-president Juscelino Kubitschek, perhaps no one would have known about this fact, as the Navy had already "questioned" me to find out how much I wanted to keep the photos from gaining any publicity. It's important to clarify that all the officers I came into contact with throughout the entire investigation were extremely kind to me, left me completely at ease, and did not object to the disclosure of the case. They only hinted that the sensationalism surrounding the case could cause panic among the population, which is why the Armed Forces were interested in not giving publicity to cases of this nature.

30/01/1967, Almiro Baraúna (UFO Magazine, 31/08/2010).

--- seg-1a2ff55f530e48f5b9693534 [needs-review] ---
The year 1986 was marked by the appearance of unidentified flying objects (UFOs) in the Brazilian skies. Not only by the famous "Official Night of the UFOs" on May 19, 1986, when several objects were observed in the region of São José dos Campos (SP) and combat aircraft took off to investigate the phenomenon, but also by numerous other occurrences recorded in different parts of the country. In the months that followed, numerous reports emerged of objects observed by civilians, aircraft pilots, air traffic controllers, and ship crews. The document presented here refers to a UFO observation report by the crews of two Brazilian ships on the night of August 12.

In addition to the reports mentioned so far, some people claim to have photographed flying saucers and other unidentified objects over time, as exemplified by an article published in the newspaper Correio Brasiliense on May 22, 1969.

--- seg-16863cee94f17f70d8c587f1 [needs-review] ---
The Unidentified Flying Object (UFO) - Objecto Voador Não Identificado (BR DFANBSB ARX) Fund from the National Archive contains records produced by the Brazilian Air Force Command. It comprises 743 records, encompassing reports, questionnaires on occurrences, correspondence, photographs, drawings, videos, audio recordings, and newspaper and magazine clippings related to objects observed in the Brazilian sky over the years.

The first occurrence in the UFO Fund dates back to 1952, while the most recent is from 2016. It is important to note that UFOs do not necessarily imply the existence of flying saucers.

2.6 The Varginha ET case and the trigger event
--- seg-263b11fadc72e49321dd6a83 [needs-review] ---
On January 20, 1996, a Saturday, young women Kátia Andrade Xavier and sisters
Liliane de Fátima Silva and Valquíria Aparecida Silva were returning home and, while cutting
through a vacant lot, they reportedly saw a creature as they crossed a plot of land between
the Jardim Andere and Santana neighborhoods in Varginha. It was around 3 PM that day when
a heavy storm had passed. Beside a wall, the three young women spotted a creature with brown,
viscous skin as if oil had been smeared on it, large red eyes, and a prominent head with three
protrusions resembling horns. Upon seeing the unidentified creature, they screamed and ran
in the same direction they had come from.

Upon arriving home, they told their mother, terrified, what had happened. The mother then
returned to the same location with a neighbor, but found nothing, only trampled grass on the
ground where the girls said they had seen the strange creature, and a strong smell of ammonia
in the air.

The girls and not even their mother called the police, firefighters, or made any movement to denounce the fact to the authorities. Until then, the girls associated the strange creature with a type of malevolent being.

===== PAGE 72 [page-606c1c7594a02acf341994c6] =====
--- seg-7ea00118733ccd235580434c [needs-review] ---

Franco Rodrigues, ufologists Marco Petit, Claudeir Covo, and A. J. Gevaerd (editor of UFO magazine) participated in the investigations at the time.

It's important to note that ufologist Ubirajara Franco Rodrigues was the main catalyst for the ET of Varginha story. However, he distanced himself from the case a few years ago and does not grant interviews or comment on the incident or subsequent events.

The Baependi case, which occurred on May 16, 1979, was also investigated and publicized by Ubirajara Franco Rodrigues. That night, farmer Arlindo Gabriel dos Santos reportedly saw three UFOs and extraterrestrial beings. At one point, Arlindo was allegedly taken inside the ship and spoke with the ETs. Unlike the Varginha case, the possible appearance and abduction in Baependi did not gain notoriety or create local identity and representations. Baependi remains known as the land of Nhá Chica, Catholic devotee Francisca de Paula de Jesus (1808-1895), who drives devotional and religious tourism in Baependi. Indeed, the pilgrimage of Nhá Chica is inscribed as Intangible Cultural Heritage of São Lourenço (MG).

However, before Ubirajara Rodrigues left the case, reports and descriptions were constantly in the media and buzzed in Varginha and among ufologists known to Ubirajara Franco Rodrigues. Ufologists began interviewing people in Varginha who claimed to have seen supernatural creatures or inexplicable events, such as the sighting of aircraft resembling UFOs. Many of these interviews from that time are available online and can be easily accessed.

Ufologists identified a series of events that occurred on that January 20, 1996, and also in the days before and after that Saturday. From these investigations, it was learned that there was strange military movement at the South Minas Regional Hospital and Humanitas Hospital. In the documentary Moment of Contact by James Fox, recently released, a military participant in the events who was at the Regional Hospital reportedly saw the creature.

During the interviews, ufologists learned that on the same day, January 20, 1996, the local Fire Department received an anonymous call at 8:30 a.m. to capture a "strange animal" in the Jardim Andere neighborhood, the same neighborhood where the girls saw the other creature in the afternoon. Witnesses reported that the military was present in large numbers in the area.

The Varginha Case

         A few days before January 20, 1996, a movement of UFOs was detected by the U.S. satellite tracking system. It was discovered that the focus of the movements was Brazil, in the southern region of Minas Gerais. The Brazilian Armed Forces were alerted.
         On January 13, 1996, a week before the main events in Varginha, ultralight pilot Carlos de Souza from São Paulo, saw a elongated, wingless artifact less than 15 km from the intersection that connects the Fernão Dias Highway to the road leading to Varginha. He visually followed the object, which seemed to be in distress, alternating altitude and route, with an opening in its front from which a mist emerged. Heading towards Belo Horizonte, after 10 km from the Varginha intersection, he observed that the object disappeared behind a hill, on the Maiolini farm. Taking the unpaved road, he saw several metallic debris being collected in the pasture by military personnel. At the site, there were two trucks, a helicopter, and an army ambulance.
         A larger piece of the damaged object, the size of a car, was placed in the truck's bed. Thousands of small pieces and debris spread across the pasture. Carlos de Souza was approached by military personnel asking him to leave the area and "forget about what he saw."

"He saw it." Returning to where he had come from, he stopped at a gas station and was again approached by two men who advised him not to discuss what he had witnessed with anyone. This testimony was not confirmed by others, remaining isolated after ufologists' investigations in farms, sites, and homes in the region. [This account is from the 2022 documentary *Moment of Contact* by James Fox, described by the person who allegedly saw the ship and its wreckage.]

January 20, 1996, around 1 a.m. Mrs. Oralina Augusta de Freitas looked out the window of the farm headquarters owned by Mr. Castilho, located 10 km from Varginha, on the highway connecting the city to Fernão Dias. The cattle burst into a run. She observed a elongated artifact releasing a mist from a part that seemed "ripped" ahead of the fuselage. She screamed for her husband, Eurico Rodrigues de Freitas, who was sleeping. Eurico also spotted the object, which hovered over the pasture at most five meters away. To cover a distance of approximately 500 meters, the object took nearly forty minutes. It then disappeared over the hill, heading towards the city.

January 20, 1996, 8:30 a.m. Six firefighters from Varginha searched for a "creature" spotted by several bystanders first at the base of a construction site in the Jardim Andere neighborhood. Later, the "creature" was seen moving slowly towards a eucalyptus tree owned by Mr. José Gomes, past the railroad track, in the large pasture behind Jardim Andere.

A man, construction worker Henrique José, witnessed the entire incident from a neighbor's house terrace and later told investigators that four firefighters cornered the creature with their nets, captured it in a wooden box, and then handed it over to the military.

After about two hours of searching, firefighters located the "creature" and caught it in a net for animal capture. A troop transport truck from the School of Sergeants of Arms was also present. The "creature" was placed in a box in the truck bed, covered with cloth or canvas, and it emitted a type of buzzing sound.

The truck sped off towards Três Corações and entered the School of Sergeants of Arms.

January 20, 1996, around 1 p.m. Mr. "X," who was walking in the area near the end of Jardim Andere and the nearby pastures, saw six men in military uniforms in a search formation, carrying automatic rifles and other weapons, exactly near the eucalyptus tree where the first capture had occurred.

Running to find a better observation point, Mr. "X" heard three rifle shots. Shortly after, he saw those men climbing the terrain before the pasture, carrying two bags. One of them had something moving inside.

January 20, 1996, 3:30 p.m. Liliane Fátima Silva (16 years old at the time), her sister Valquíria Aparecida Silva (14), and Kátia Andrade Xavier (22) tried to take a shorter route to the Santana neighborhood, next to Jardim Andere. As they passed by a mechanic's shop on Benevenuto Braz Vieira Street, two blocks above the pasture and eucalyptus tree, they were surprised to see a "creature" with skin that shone as if it were oiled. The "creature," with red and rounded eyes, jumping out of its orbits without a pupil or iris, crouched next to the mechanic's shop wall. It slowly turned its head towards the girls. They don't remember the characteristics of its mouth and nose.

With purple veins bulging at the height of its thighs, long and thin arms, and enormous feet, it had a prominent skull with three protuberances, giving the impression of...

horns. The three girls scream and run towards your residence, arriving in tears and panic.
January 20th, 1996, 5:30 PM. A torrential downpour with hail scares the population. After the storm, two military personnel arrive at the site where the three girls had spotted the strange being. Something with a head, torso, and limbs passes in front of the car and tries to hide in the grass.
Police officer Marco Eli Chereze, 23, runs and grabs the creature by the arm, placing it in the backseat of the car and taking it to the Regional Hospital. The movement by the police, firefighters, and military causes hysteria in the hospital, where patients are transferred from wards and staff instructed to remain silent.
January 21st, midnight. The "being" is transferred to the Humanitas Hospital in an ambulance, without fanfare. From Humanitas, the "material" is removed the following Monday, January 22nd, around 5:30 PM.
The "creature" is dead. The transport begins with the placement of the "being" in a wooden box, covered with plastic tarpaulin and placed in the bed of a troop transport truck, with its rear against the hospital's back gate. Several military vehicles and some civilian ones make up the convoy. People dressed in white and medical masks are present.
The being exudes an unbearable smell of ammonia.
The convoy heads to the city of Três Corações and inside the School of Arms Sergeants.
January 23rd, 5 AM. The same convoy leaves for Campinas-SP, carrying two loads. The body removed from the Humanitas Hospital and a ventilated box containing something alive. After a stop at the Preparatory School for Cadets in Campinas, other military vehicles transport the "material" to the UNICAMP facilities. For a week, a team of researchers and scientists conducts analyses, examinations, and tests on the "creatures." Among them is the renowned forensic pathologist Prof. Fortunato Badan Palhares. Work is done on the "material," even in a modern laboratory.
The fate of these "beings" after Campinas is unknown.
On February 7th, 1996, Marco Eli Chereze undergoes surgery to remove a pustule from his right armpit. With severe pain and the onset of paralysis, he is admitted and dies eight days later from septic pulmonary thromboembolism, as concluded by the autopsy. His blood tests positive for 8% of an unknown culture, as the police officer was a victim of widespread infection.
On April 21st, 1996, Mrs. Terezinha Gallo Clepf, in a restaurant inside Varginha's zoo, saw a strange creature, identical to the one seen by the young women, with one notable difference: it had a kind of helmet on its head. At that time, two deer, a tapir, a jaguar, and a blue macaw mysteriously died. After the analyses, no cause for these deaths was discovered.

The official version - On March 11, 1997, Maurício Antônio Santos, 44, commander of the 24th
        Military Police Battalion in Varginha, testified to the Three Hearts ESA that the
        alleged extraterrestrial observed by the girls was, in fact, a thirty-something Varginha
        citizen known as "Mudinho." Suffering from mental issues, Mudinho would often crouch
        along walls in the Andere neighborhood, collecting cigarette butts and other objects from
        the ground.
        It's more likely that this citizen, probably dirty due to the heavy rains, seen crouching
        next to a wall, was mistaken by three terrified girls for a 'space creature,' the commander's
        testimony states. The intense activity of firefighters and military police (he does not
        mention the Army) is attributed to the heavy rains that day.
        Ufologists argue that on the day of the ET's appearance, there was also heavy traffic of
        Three Hearts ESA trucks in Varginha. Military officials counter that the
        vehicles were indeed moved, but for maintenance at the Mercedes Benz dealership in the
        city. An invoice for 432 reais for alignment and balancing services on eight trucks was
        attached to the case files.
        In addition to stating that the rain had not yet started when the girls saw the alleged ET,
        Covo argues that the Mercedes Benz dealership was closed on weekends. January 20, 1996,
        was a Saturday. Ufologists also claim: in the IPM, the address of the "Automáco
        Comercial e Importadora LTDA." company, which appears on a government financial
        administration system (SIAFI) invoice, is different from the invoice present in the
        investigation. All documented ufo cases in Brazil are listed in a Brazilian Aeroespacial
        Defense Command (COMDABRA) table, which quantifies the number of records per year
        since 1952. Interestingly, 1996 is the year with the highest number of ufo reports: 65.
        The case remains a complex puzzle, filled with contradictions and discrepancies.

A summary of the Varginha ET Case

The Varginha Case is now known worldwide and is recognized by ufologists as the most significant case in Brazil and this century. Here is a summary of the story: On January 13, 2006, the couple Eurico Rodrigues de Freitas and Oralina Augusta de Freitas, around 1:30 a.m., saw a small, submarine-shaped craft, about the size of a minibus, with a large hole on one end, emitting white smoke, without noise, without lighting, and flying slowly. The craft appeared to have difficulty flying and was hovering over a farm 10 kilometers from Varginha's center.

===== PAGE 87 [page-ec1aea6efe24ebbb5ccd761f] =====
--- seg-24a81538c5d787de675a2abf [needs-review] ---
Registration Process for intangible property: "The E.T. Case and the Varginha Identity"

Year 2023 | Fiscal Year 2025
--- seg-8dbc13af9f564246c2eca766 [machine-unreviewed] ---
In 2010, a dossier was revealed with an investigation conducted by the army in 1997, which
concluded that what the girls had seen was a local resident, nicknamed Mudinho, who had
mental health issues. G1 reported the story in 2010, as follows:
--- seg-a6a08b35102fab39c4366627 [needs-review] ---
10/19/2010 10:13 - Updated on 10/19/2010 10:14
The Story of the Varginha Alien Gains a New Version
According to the magazine Isto é, a military report disputes the existence. A document completed in 1997 was only recently released.
From G1 MG with information from EPTV. 2010.00/00:00
After 14 years, the case of the Varginha alien is still surrounded by controversies and sparks the curiosity of many. A report from the Brazilian Army, done in 1997 and only recently released, shows that no extraterrestrial being ever appeared in southern Minas Gerais.
The magazine ‘Isto é’ published a report on the police investigation that looked into the involvement of the Army, Military Police, and Fire Department in the Varginha case. The result makes clear that there was no capture of an extraterrestrial being on January 20, 1996.
The article with the title “The Official Story of the Varginha Alien” reveals, according to the magazine, what are said to be the only two documents produced by the government to investigate the occurrences and the accusations made against military personnel who allegedly participated in the case. The inquiry, opened and completed in 1997, has 357 pages and details testimonies. According to the report, Lieutenant-Colonel Lúcio Carlos Pereira, in charge of the inquiry, concluded that what actually happened was a misunderstanding. The text states that a citizen, probably muddy due to the rain and seen crouched next to a wall, was mistaken for a space creature by three girls. A photographic study allegedly simulated the similarity between a man and the alleged alien. A resident of the city named Luiz Antônio, now 46 years old, is said to have been the man seen on the vacant lot at that time.
The results of an investigation conducted at the Sergeant's School of Arms in Três Corações in May 1996 were attached to the inquiry. Ubirajara Rodrigues, a ufologist from Varginha, was heard in the investigation. According to him, the inquiry should be taken into account, but researchers cannot rule out hypotheses. “Every ufological phenomenon today has an entire aspect of myth, it is mythical, let’s say that. But I cannot today, after the evolution of research, affirm or deny absolutely anything about the Varginha case. We have been revisiting the case for several years and it will last for a long time,” said Rodrigues.
--- seg-312740e1ce060c5785ad27b7 [needs-review] ---
The report titled "Exército Documents" was drafted by Vinicius Proba dos Santos, and comprises two volumes totaling 367 pages.
--- seg-34f6058a43352d6e1ca41bbb [needs-review] ---

3. Detailed description of the cultural asset
--- seg-b2527ab57639bd3f4d78b28f [needs-review] ---
The generative event, occurring in Varginha in January 1996, had international repercussions and made the Varginha ET Case known and generated various representations. These representations are materialized in buildings, bus stop shelters, and distinct furniture, in crafts, sculptures, animations, books, documentaries, tiles, among others.

As known among cultural heritage scholars, one of the possibilities for recognizing cultural heritage, both immaterial and material, is through associations and representations made about it over time, through music, poetry, literature, images, souvenirs, cartoons, statuettes, monuments, buildings, paintings, magazine and newspaper publications, both printed and online, among others. The Varginha ET Case, as demonstrated in this study, has hundreds of representations and recreations made since 1996 until today. This keeps the asset constantly alive in the collective imagination and offers possibilities for continuity.

The symbolic values and images associated with an asset, which inspire distinct representations, are recognized by UNESCO for the classification of an asset. One of the criteria highlighted by UNESCO for the recognition of assets and their inscription on the Representative Lists of the World Heritage is that the asset is directly or materially associated with living events or traditions, ideas, beliefs, or works of art and literature.

These associated values should project the culture and relevant aspects of the place represented. When representations of what would be aliens appear in animations, cartoons, or books, drinking coffee and eating cheese bread, it refers to the local Varginha culture and Brazilian culture as a whole. These representations, indeed, make Varginha recognized through them. If a person is photographed under the replica of the spacecraft, installed by COPASA in the city center, it's not necessary for the person to describe where they are for it to be recognized that they are in Varginha. In other words, the representation, in this case, the mockup of the spacecraft, takes the place of the represented thing, which in this case is Varginha and the relation with the ET's history. The same occurs with ET representations in the form of sculptures.
--- seg-a3953fdf94175cf2c0ab46ab [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | Mayor: Verdi Lúcio Melo | Sector Head: Danielle de Souza Guimarães
                     Responsible Sector: Cultural Heritage Technical Coordination
              Praça Matheus Tavares, 121 | Neighborhood: Center | Zip Code: 37002-320 | Tel.: (35) 3690-2718

===== PAGE 91 [page-fef9aa7643803edcb2c617b7] =====
--- seg-194c434cda37f0bf884eca1e [needs-review] ---
Registration Process for intangible property: "The E.T. Case and the Varginha Identity"

Municipality of Varginha | MG

Year 2023 | Exercise 2025
--- seg-7dd990c10e0ce8232a35b87e [needs-review] ---
The consistency of representations, discourses, and images created and recreated around the Varginha ET case has kept the memory of the event alive, even for those who have never been to Varginha. In the national and global imagination, Varginha is the municipality where the strange creatures allegedly emerged in January 1996, identified by ufologists as extraterrestrial beings. In this sense, the ET is from Varginha, it is in Varginha, even if the supposed appearance event only happened once. It's as if Varginha's territory is the territory of the ETs, or at least those that allegedly emerged there. It is very likely that the mystery and secrecy imposed by the authorities around the case, particularly the military, fuels imagination, and new representations and narratives are created over time. This gives continuity to the positive and promotes its permanence in the local, national, and international imagination.
--- seg-332387ea11816e3ced1583e9 [machine-unreviewed] ---

Based on this idea of representations and how the Varginha ET case materialized in the municipality and beyond, generating identity, recreations, and possibilities of continuity, we present these materializations.
--- seg-a99e7513ef475e978f81dfc7 [machine-unreviewed] ---
1) COPASA water tank - Elevated water reservoir
      Praça Marechal Floriano, Center, Varginha - MG
--- seg-e93d1bd8cf82c9732ff68e42 [needs-review] ---
The most iconic asset related to the ET Case is a massive water reservoir suspended, built by COPASA in 2003, in Praça Marechal Floriano, in the urban center of Varginha. The project for the work dates back to 2001, but construction began in 2003. The structure consists of a cylindrical column supporting a space ship, similar to representations of UFOs. Dimensions of the suspended reservoir: height = 20 meters and water storage capacity of 100,000 liters. The project is by architect Paulo Eduardo Antunes Corrêa.

In the square where the reservoir was installed, a garden was created around it, and a sculpture depicting an ET was erected. The sculpture was made by plastic artist Michele Tommaso Vanzetti, interviewed during the investigation process for registration. Michele told us that the sculpture/statue of the so-called "ETzinho" was based on a drawing by cartoonist and illustrator Paulo Antônio de Carvalho.

The initiative to have the "ET's Ship Monument" built, as the reservoir became known, was then-Mayor Mauro Tadeu Teixeira (1953-2010), who served two consecutive terms from 2001 to 2008. With the need for a water reservoir by COPASA, the municipality's water supplier, Mayor Mauro Teixeira negotiated with the company, using the Varginha ET Case story in a clever way, and thus built the reservoir with shapes resembling a large space ship. Perhaps he couldn't have imagined that the suspended water reservoir, in the shape of a ship, would become the most visited and photographed monument related to the Varginha ET Case.
--- seg-a30683dfe40a6f7368447bc5 [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | Mayor: Verdi Lúcio Melo | Sector Head: Danielle de Souza Guimarães
                     Responsible Sector: Cultural Heritage Technical Coordination
              Praça Matheus Tavares, 121 | Neighborhood: Center | Zip Code: 37002-320 | Tel.: (35) 3690-2718
--- seg-623f117b760944530dd70b5e [machine-unreviewed] ---

===== PAGE 94 [page-9b4ea93362a733c506468402] =====
--- seg-889617d1c5b14989ad9951ee [needs-review] ---
Registration Process for intangible property: "The E.T. Case and the Varginha Identity"

Municipality of Varginha | MG

Year 2023 | Exercise 2025
--- seg-cacff740887749e07583116e [needs-review] ---
The construction of the water reservoir took place in 2003. The contracted contractor was COMIN Engenharias, supervised by COPASA and the Municipal Municipality as a partner. In 2005, the unusual water tank received lighting. The Monument has special nighttime lighting, creating a very futuristic effect, giving the impression of movement and color change, as in a science fiction. From almost all points in the city, it is possible to visualize the large Ship suspended in the air.
--- seg-b0a1dfbb51be38e2253ef49f [needs-review] ---
Image 25: The construction of the Water Tank, in 2003, and the installation of shelters at bus stops. Official document of the Municipality, December 30, 2003. Collection of the Varginha Cultural Foundation
--- seg-ad7402053fcae40f1fb7f5d2 [needs-review] ---
The Monument stands out both for the local population and for tourists who often take pictures of it or under the famous ship. The structure is located in the center of the urban area of the municipality, near Getúlio Vargas Square, Marshal Floriano Square, the highest point of Major Venâncio Avenue, one of the main access roads to the city. The asset has been listed as a Heritage of Varginha since 2010.

2) Little ET, a character created by Paulo Antônio de Carvalho
--- seg-02112b6f6aa88e97478792d8 [needs-review] ---
The green-colored ET image, in a more playful form, was first created by cartoonist Paulo Antônio de Carvalho. Indeed, the name "ETzinho" (little ET) is a trademark of the cartoonist and illustrator Paulo Antônio. It is his drawings of the green ET that were reproduced as sculptures alongside the ship, or water reservoir of COPASA, and other sculptures scattered throughout the municipality.

This representation of the ET by Paulo Antonio de Carvalho is one of the most famous and widespread both within and outside the municipality of Varginha.

3) Character: Ginho, the Varginha ET and the Comic Book Ginho
--- seg-434d7316f4b49c75c65fcaae [needs-review] ---
Ginho, a fictional character representing the Varginha alien, was created in 2007 by cartoonist Márcio Baraldi for Revista UFO. It was Mário Baraldi who designed the green-represented alien, instead of the brown representation described by the girls who claimed to have seen the creature. The idea was to make the alien character more playful and add a touch of humor to the seriousness of the UFO magazine.

In October 2016, the cartoonist launched the album Ginho, the Varginha Alien, in partnership between Revista UFO and GRRR (Gibi Raivoso Radical e Revolucionário), an independent label by the author.
--- seg-df8a31cc8542b35c770778a5 [needs-review] ---

4) ET Memorial in Varginha
--- seg-b3236247dc3d32b5f067f7bf [needs-review] ---
In 2022, the ET Memorial was inaugurated on Avenida Maria Paiva Pinto, number 105, in Vila Paiva. The memorial is constructed in the form of an architectural oval structure resembling a representation of a UFO. In the same year, the Tourism Secretariat moved into the new building.

A nearby astronomical planetarium was also built, resembling a UFO. Additionally, a water reservoir shaped like a rocket was constructed. The Memorial and Planetarium are open to the public every day of the week from 9 a.m. to 6 p.m., with the last session at the planetarium starting at 5:30 p.m.

At the time of the inauguration in November 2022, the following note was published on Madeira's blog:

"ET Memorial Inauguration to Take Place Next Tuesday

After years of waiting, the ET Memorial will finally be inaugurated next Tuesday, November 8, after 13 years. The project was announced in 2008 with an initial investment of R$ 1.2 million. Initially, the delivery deadline was set for 2013, but it suffered several extensions. Construction even had to be halted when the responsible company went bankrupt. Currently, the building houses the Municipal Tourism Secretariat, which is expected to remain there after the inauguration.

The top of the Memorial is intended to house a UFOology museum, which, according to the Tourism Secretariat, will feature interactive panels, monitors telling the story of the case, and an observatory with a telescope."

The Memorial is located on top of Vila Paiva, near Copasa.
--- seg-38ea8ecf98c812ebdc749720 [needs-review] ---
The construction of the ET Memorial project was announced in 2008, with funding secured directly from the Ministry of Tourism. In 2010, the city hall selected the land for construction. In 2011, the contractor responsible declared bankruptcy, halting the project. It was resumed in 2013/2014. The project is designed by architect Salvador Homero Maia Silva, a former employee of Varginha City Hall.

At the time of the project's completion in 2021, Mayor Vérdi Melo stated:

"The completion of the Memorial's construction celebrates a project achieved through the efforts of many, a bold endeavor long awaited by the people of Varginha and other states, even from abroad, as it is directly linked to the ET case, one of the most significant UFO events in the world."

To enhance the initial project, the Secretary of Tourism and Commerce, Barry Charles Sobrinho, envisioned bringing the Planetarium that is now attached to the Memorial, where educational and cultural films with references to the ET, galaxies, and ufology will be shown for children and adults. The films will be displayed in 360° and will last up to 30 minutes. Also present at the unveiling were the young people who saw the ET in the afternoon of January 20, 1996. The unveiling of the ET Memorial is a demonstration of respect for the public resources invested there, which was not insignificant. Federal and municipal resources. The construction was controversial from the beginning, either due to the purpose of the resource or the delay and suspicions surrounding the work. But finally, the work was completed, and the site can have various utilities, and it still has the planetarium that can be used by various secretariats such as culture and education."

The ET Memorial was named 'Radialista Galvão Conde' in honor of the journalist who passed away at 84 years old in 2017. Galvão Conde dedicated himself to communication and promoting Varginha's name on radios.

5) Construction of Varginha City Hall
--- seg-611c7a65c85ebc6833fd36a5 [needs-review] ---
The building that houses the Varginha City Hall was also constructed with elements
alluding to the ET Case. Located at Rua Júlio Marcelini, n. 50, Bairro Vila Paiva, the
Mauro Tadeu Teixeira Administrative Center - Varginha Municipal Hall was inaugurated on
June 15, 2012. The name is a tribute to the late former mayor who served from 2001 to
2008 and passed away in 2010. The Administrative Center or Municipal Hall is located in
the former CBC - heavy industries facilities, where the SEMEL sports center and the
Department of Public Works are also located. The architect responsible for the panoramic
elevator and the dome was Giovani Oliveira Bernardes.

6) ET Square
--- seg-f32937deb7ad08bd30f33c4a [needs-review] ---
In the ET Square, as Praça José de Rezende Paiva is known, located in the central area of Varginha, there are two representations, approximately 2 meters tall, made based on the descriptions of the ET given by the girls who reportedly saw the creature in 1996. The statues impress everyone passing through the city due to their size and type of representation.

Sculptor and prosthetist Mauri Bomfim told us that the design is his and that the execution was done by the plastic artist Cléo Nogueira. The execution date is October 2004, according to a note published in the Official Organ.

7) The E.T.'s route
--- seg-57b02e0bf9c125fac4c6c94c [needs-review] ---
The ET's Route from Varginha was part of a photographic exhibition titled "Photographic Exhibition: Varginha, City of the ET," a project supported by the Aldir Blanc Law, the State Government of Minas Gerais, the Special Secretariat of Culture, and the Ministry of Tourism of the Federal Government.

In addition to the photographic exhibition, the author created a map route featuring representations of E.T.s or ships in Varginha's urban area. The map is accessible via the link: https://memorialdoet.com.br/rota-do-et-varginha-mapa-pontos-turisticos/

However, this Route is also used by the Tourism Secretariat to point out locations of works, sculptures, etc., that identify the ET Case of Varginha in the municipality.

Novo Horizonte Municipal Park
Pasteur Avenue - Catanduvas Park, Varginha - MG, 37006
--- seg-0294bd30614b868a785150d3 [needs-review] ---
Located near the Zoobotanical Park and covering an area of 27,000 square meters (almost three hectares), the Novo Horizonte Park was created in 1987 based on a design by architect Aristides Martins. The park features a representation of an E.T. at the entrance and a shelter for ducks near the park's lake. In 2018, there was a small E.T. similar to the one in Praça Marechal Floriano, but it is no longer there today. The Novo Horizonte Park has been municipally protected since March 3, 2000.

Municipal Zoobotanical Park Dr. Mário Frota
Petrópolis Street - Petrópolis Garden, Varginha - MG, 37006-650
--- seg-6cb9b380b8a6070bf7da6e21 [needs-review] ---
The Municipal Zoobotanical Park was established in 1963 through the initiative of Dr. Mário Frota, who purchased land to house the animals from the Gran Circo Africano. The park's inauguration took place in 1966 with the construction of its first animal enclosures, and in 1967 it was declared of public utility. The Zoobotanical Park has been municipal heritage since March 3, 2000.

At the Varginha Zoo, it is believed that there was a UFO sighting shortly after the girls' encounter in January 1996.

--- seg-7ab6efdc586267248e9e9fd4 [needs-review] ---

10) Local crafts based on E.T. representations
--- seg-19e32bcd81b0e7f58f50b3e7 [needs-review] ---
At a meeting with the craftswomen of Varginha, gathered in local associations, namely: the Varginha Mineira Arts and Crafts Association (AMINARTE), the Varginha Association of Popular Craftsmen and Artists (ASSOART), the Varginha Association of Retirees and Pensioners (AAPV), and the Varginha Association for Creative Economy (AVEC), all agreed to the registration and expressed enthusiasm for the idea of preserving the patrimonial value of the site.

The registration will enable mechanisms to foster sustainable development and boost sales of pieces related to the Varginha ET case. It is worth noting that the Convention on Intangible Cultural Heritage of UNESCO reaffirms "the importance of intangible cultural heritage as a source of cultural diversity and a guarantee of sustainable development, as highlighted in the UNESCO Recommendation on the Safeguarding of Traditional and Popular Culture, 1989, as well as in the UNESCO Universal Declaration on Cultural Diversity, 2001, and the Istanbul Declaration, 2002, adopted by the Third Ministerial Round Table on Culture." (Unesco, Safeguarding Intangible Cultural Heritage, 2003).

===== PAGE 129 [page-e0e8d9fc14ce76870fefbad5] =====
--- seg-fbb4b5b407361eaa50964157 [needs-review] ---
Registration Process for intangible property: "The E.T. Case and the Varginha Identity"

Municipality of Varginha | MG

Year 2023 | Exercise 2025
--- seg-4c7cd2c528e0a5eeb1fc91f1 [machine-unreviewed] ---
11) Statues with replicas of what is imagined to be the face of an alien (ET)
--- seg-cd156091ac154bfb6d5fcd74 [needs-review] ---
Statues with replicas of the creature described by the girls were scattered throughout the city. The design of the little alien installed next to the COPASA water tank or reservoir was created by cartoonist and illustrator Paulo Antônio de Carvalho in a work executed by plastic artist Michele Tommaso Vanzetti.

Michele Vanzetti told us that he carved the piece on-site where it was installed, and that the head was the most challenging part to make. It was created elsewhere and later brought to the square. The head was made with fiberglass to keep it from becoming too heavy, and the rest of the body was made with cement plaster molded. The sculpture is 2 meters tall and took approximately two months to complete. The stone on which the sculpture was installed was donated by the Varginha Country Club and brought to the site specifically for the installation of the little alien replica.

The mayor who commissioned the work was Mauro Teixeira, and his wife, Geisa Gomes Pereira Teixeira, is said to have suggested creating a more playful model of an alien in green, distinct from the older brown representations, according to Michele Tommaso Vanzetti's account. It was during this time that they also installed the little alien from the CDCA - Child and Adolescent Development Center.

Michele also mentioned that the map of Minas Gerais positioned on the chest of the little alien was stolen twice and later recreated. The previous two replicas were made of glass. Finally, they made a replica of the Minas Gerais map using plaster and it is the one currently on-site, representing the little alien's heart.
--- seg-34d08457194becc87413f204 [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | Mayor: Verdi Lúcio Melo | Sector Head: Danielle de Souza Guimarães
                     Responsible Sector: Cultural Heritage Technical Coordination
              Praça Matheus Tavares, 121 | Neighborhood: Center | Zip Code: 37002-320 | Tel.: (35) 3690-2718

12) The sculptor Mauri Bomfim Gabriel
--- seg-4bc4096adf493f55684c2d95 [needs-review] ---
Sculptor Mauri Bomfim, 63, was one of the first to reproduce in miniatures
representations of what is assumed to be the appearance of the Varginha alien.
--- seg-0f9b87acd3a36df95b7180b8 [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | Mayor: Verdi Lúcio Melo | Sector Head: Danielle de Souza Guimarães

===== PAGE 131 [page-f4edd17dcff67dafe5b0a2ef] =====
--- seg-fef794d39734a3990e83085c [needs-review] ---
Registration Process for intangible property: "The E.T. Case and the Varginha Identity"

Municipality of Varginha | MG

Year 2023 | Exercise 2025
--- seg-cbd731d9e6357dcd41181d91 [needs-review] ---
Mauri told us that he is a prosthetist and that as soon as the ET story broke in the media,
he became interested in it. One day, shortly after the events were reported, he was in his dental
prosthetics lab and the power went out and he couldn't continue his work, so he started sculpting
a representative image of the ET in plaster. Until then, he had never sculpted any kind of
representation. For him, the first representation was the most faithful to the descriptions of the
girls. Later, he created new, more playful forms and others that appeal to all kinds of audiences,
but always referencing the descriptions of the girls who had seen the creature.
--- seg-23ea45f979beabb87dcfb1fc [needs-review] ---
Image 96: Early representations of the Varginha alien created by Mauri Bomfim, still in 1996. Collection of Mauri Bomfim.
--- seg-10f4c42d0beb2dffc1e19278 [needs-review] ---
It is possible to notice that these early representations were quite faithful to the description of the girls who witnessed the creature. Later, Mauri improved the pieces and created distinct models, more playful and aesthetically more accepted by society.

Mauri considers that the story of the ET "fell from the sky" for him, as from then on he began creating representations and making miniatures and full-size pieces that gained worldwide recognition. He accounts that he discovered art through the ET story. The artistic work derived from the ET representations serves as occupational therapy and to financially support his family needs. Mauri stated: "The pieces I create are generally to express something to elevate the ET's and Varginha's City's name, promote tourism, the profession, and even in the education field, to discuss prejudices such as racism."
--- seg-3db75911f594d72d7b91fad6 [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | Mayor: Verdi Lúcio Melo | Sector Head: Danielle de Souza Guimarães
                     Responsible Sector: Cultural Heritage Technical Coordination
              Praça Matheus Tavares, 121 | Neighborhood: Center | Zip Code: 37002-320 | Tel.: (35) 3690-2718
--- seg-a40da0367f2ffc6743dbe9b5 [machine-unreviewed] ---
Page 131 of 403

===== PAGE 132 [page-b6b5ca229ea25ec8bac203a3] =====
--- seg-d7bd832a138742f0b4cde409 [needs-review] ---
Registration Process for intangible property: "The E.T. Case and the Varginha Identity"

Municipality of Varginha | MG

Year 2023 | Fiscal Year 2025
--- seg-e3f131253029d8c2e4aba0cc [needs-review] ---
From this, it is possible to create versions that highlight agriculture, the environment, reading, among others."

Mauri has approximately 30 distinct models representing the Varginha Alien. The first time he molded it was in plaster, but he has also carved it in wood, clay, stone, metal, among others. He considers that he has experimented with all possible raw materials to create pieces representing the Varginha Alien.

He only carves and creates representations of the Varginha Alien, nothing else. Mauri also told us that pieces he has made have gone to various places around the world. He even made a piece for the Roswell Museum, which was taken there by ufologist Vitório Pacaccini.

The pieces are always made to order, and he says he doesn't always manage to meet the demand. In Brazil, he sends them to stores that order his work in other states like Bahia, São Paulo, Paraná, and cities like Taubaté, São Thomé das Letras, São José dos Campos, among others. Outside Brazil, he remembers sending pieces to Canada and the United States. Some time ago, some acquaintances approached him and said they had seen his pieces in a store in Miami, USA.

The sculptor has also made numerous pieces that were given as gifts to artists such as Faustão, Jô Soares, Sandy, Junior, Milton Nascimento, and Ana Maria Braga. Some of these artists have presented the pieces he sent on their shows. One of the first to show his pieces was Ratinho on his show 26 years ago. His pieces have also appeared on the program "A Fazenda."

He has also created Aliens representing football teams. These are very famous and in high demand. Some coaches like Parreira and Felipão have been given pieces made by him. Mauri said it's not him who gives the gifts, but someone who orders and buys the pieces to give as gifts to artists and celebrities or take abroad.
--- seg-a9ba41974c6c91a31a5f0ad0 [needs-review] ---

--- seg-4f004c5e624d78f731428f85 [needs-review] ---
Mauri Bomfim believes his actions were among those that kept the story alive not only in the municipality of Varginha but beyond. He considers that he sold, at the beginning, about a thousand souvenirs per month. Currently, he sells around five thousand pieces per month, including keychains, cachaca bottles, full-size replicas, among others.

In his home, he has various pieces measuring about one and a half meters, made of fiber, that he made for himself. These pieces are dressed in characteristic clothes of each era, such as the World Cup era, represented below. Other full-size versions, when commissioned, are made for sales.

14) Games based on the Varginha Case
--- seg-816ddcb848598010b9be037d [machine-unreviewed] ---
➢ Varginha Incident
--- seg-c20c4a990938fb3bc77c1d24 [needs-review] ---
In September 1998, two years after the Varginha ET incident, Perceptum, in partnership with software companies in Brazil and Midas Interactive in Europe, Asia, and other countries in South America, released the game "Incident in Varginha." This game is considered "the most controversial video game in Brazil."
--- seg-1cff87c9cfbddd234894ceed [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | Mayor: Verdi Lúcio Melo | Sector Head: Danielle de Souza Guimarães

===== PAGE 139 [page-fe4678c36ecbfe8d3ca52762] =====
--- seg-d7ba2fc9122a259d261af9b1 [needs-review] ---
Registration Process for intangible property: "The E.T. Case and the Varginha Identity"

Municipality of Varginha | MG

Year 2023 | Exercise 2025
--- seg-853ad32eae49c183a7704974 [needs-review] ---
"The first major game production developed in Brazil" and "the first Brazilian FPS," as well as
the game that gave the starting shot to more elaborate national game productions.
In other countries, the game is marketed as Alien Anarchy or Misión Alien. To develop the game,
Perceptum developers (Marcos Cuzziol, Odair Gaspar) traveled to the city of Varginha, where they
spoke with residents to get a firsthand account of the case. In addition to this research, they also
stated that they studied ufological mythology in general so that "the backstory could make sense
to those who know the subject."
According to information from Uol Notícias' website, the game's music was composed by
Fábio Cardelli, and its setting is in Brazilian cities such as Rio de Janeiro, São Paulo, and
Varginha. The game was in production for two years and four months. 2,000 copies were sold in
the country and 20,000 in the rest of the world. After its release, Perceptum signed an agreement
with the US Special Forces Training Center and NovaLogic to use the software to train Delta
Force soldiers.
--- seg-d852f18ed7b09d0072dc2e4f [needs-review] ---
Bitnamic Software game. Available at: https://www.bitnamic.com.br/product/varginha-incident/

16) UFO magazines
--- seg-2eec31574777431d8f5ede1a [needs-review] ---
Ademar José Gevaerd founded the UFO Magazine in 1985. The magazine is considered the only one on Ufology existing in the country, with almost 40 years of duration, and the oldest in circulation worldwide.

Since 1996, various editions of the UFO Magazine have been dedicated to publishing about the Varginha E.T. Case. In 1996, under the title "Alien captured in Minas Gerais and authorities deny," issue 43 published an extensive article about the appearance of a strange creature in Varginha.

18) Works of fiction inspired by the Varginha E.T. Case
--- seg-905b2c5485ebb238483829e1 [needs-review] ---
Book: Et of Varginha. The Et of Brazil, 2016, by Arlindo Cruz
--- seg-92022a28f3bf0b53971d99e5 [needs-review] ---
Synopsis: This work is not a UFOological study of the incident. It is a retelling of it, in the
romance fiction category, starring two young people, one human and one alien, who are
portrayed naively and recklessly. Recounting the case as a love story seems impossible,
nonsensical. But, in the author's view, everything happened for love, and he explains why the
ETs came to Varginha, forcing them to take such risks among its inhabitants. Thus, the story
begins in the rural area of Varginha and then moves to its urban center. The plot, initially, is
set in a forest and a village of simple-minded mineiros and nordestinos. Fictitious, it takes
place on January 19, 1996, one day before the "D-day". The events resulting from the romantic
involvement between the two different young people allow them to live an adventure-filled
and passionate journey while escaping those who are pursuing them: ETs, the army, the
farmer, and his gang. The urban plot, with the "D-day" and the young people's antics in
Varginha, was constructed based on witness descriptions, reports (from TV, magazines, and
newspapers), documentaries, official and unofficial reports, ufologist books, and, most
importantly, the account of the three girls' encounter with an ET. All the events occur within
48 hours and are retold in a timeless and sequential manner. Everything is interwoven, without
boredom, in the romance. This work is filled with romantic scenes, plenty of action, adventure,
suspense, and touches of humor.

French book: UFO Contacts in Brazil, by Thiago Luiz TICCHETTI
--- seg-00174641f0fd81ab57382e92 [needs-review] ---
Synopsis: Brazil is one of the countries with the highest number of UFO cases in the world. The incidents that occur in the country are among the most spectacular in the ufology literature, and this book will present the reader with some of the most fascinating close encounter cases, many of which have never been published abroad before, with numerous details, photos, and illustrations. It covers well-known cases, such as the Varginha incident in January 1996, which was a catalyst for the first global ufology forum in Brasilia in 1997, attended by the French author and speaker Gildas Bourdais. The book devotes significant space to the 1977 Operation Prato: from the late 1970s, particularly in the Colares region, fishermen and farmers were pursued at night by small UFOs nicknamed "chupas," lifting them off the ground and burning them with light rays. A military investigation took place in the Colares region (Pará state), known as "Operation Prato" and led by Colonel Hollanda. The latter confirmed the events in 1997. A significant declassification of UFO archives was carried out by the military around 2007, concerning this case among others. The Crab Island case was the subject of a lengthy investigation by American ufologist Bob Pratt, who lived in the country for a long time. This tragic case saw a 22-year-old man lose his life on April 25, 1977, under unexplained circumstances to this day. The book also includes a complete account of the Antonio Villas-Boas case, a historical abduction case that occurred in 1957 in Minas Gerais. Also featured is the Ubatuba case (1957), a genuine UFO crash studied by official American services and commented on in major ufology journals worldwide. The book contains several other lesser-known UFO crash cases. The "Night of the Chupas" is one of the most remarkable chapters, detailing the mysterious encounters that terrorized the residents of Colares.

Registration Process for intangible property: "The E.T. Case and the Varginha Identity"

--- seg-d9c7f29f5117f2cbab54f579 [needs-review] ---
19) Characters and versions based on the Varginha E.T.
--- seg-7e6e22358253c555da1f0fc2 [needs-review] ---
Starting in 1996, various representations were made to illustrate how the E.T. from Varginha, who became part of the local folklore, might have appeared.

The first depiction of E.T. was created by cinematographer Vinício Cunha of TV Alterosa, based on the accounts of the three young women who claimed to have seen the creature, at the request of ufologist Ubirajara Rodrigues, shortly after the initial reports of the case. Subsequently, numerous other representations emerged, including those of ETzinho (Little E.T.) and Ginho, the Varginha E.T. These gained popularity for presenting a more playful version of E.T., in green, appealing more generally to children, from around 2005.

Over time, dozens of other versions were produced, some more serious, others more playful, some more frightening, and others more cheerful. Notable artists who contributed to these various representations of the Varginha E.T. and are highlighted in this Dossier include cartoonists, illustrators, and graphic artists: Mauri Bomfim, Paulo Antônio de Carvalho, Michele Vanzetti, Mário Baraldi, Vinício Cunha, Elizabeth Aparecida Rodrigues Silva, Flávio Mattos, Gleiber Antônio Piva, and others. We apologize in advance for any notable omissions.

20) Other distinct ET models from those representing Varginha
--- seg-95479d3a527b5a3c032ee345 [needs-review] ---

21) A Varginha ET becomes a character in the animated film "BugiGangue no Espaço"
--- seg-ba0cbde79fd5a98662f2f16d [needs-review] ---
In the 2017 animation "BugiGangue no Espaço" by filmmaker Ale McHaddo, the Varginha alien is one of the characters and is depicted, in a caricatural manner, wearing boots and a Minas Gerais (Brazilian state) accent.
--- seg-ddf59bcbafa7e16ff17e4760 [needs-review] ---
Produced by 44Toons, the extraterrestrial will be a treasure hunter who joins other characters on a space journey. The character's attire is quite caricatured, with boots, a yellow uniform, and a hat. According to the producer, the film will be the first Brazilian 3D animation. Attention is also given to the voice-overs, which will be done by Maísa Silva and Danilo Gentili.
--- seg-1830c45a982e32e93f3d061a [machine-unreviewed] ---
"The creation of the ET was based on witness reports and materials about the case published in the media. The expectation is that the extraterrestrial will be a __UFO_PROTECTED_000__."

In 2020, during a Heritage Education Workshop with Varginha's Heritage Councilors, they created perception maps. By analyzing the drawings produced by the councilors, it was noticed that two significant cultural references repeatedly appeared on the maps, among which we highlight: "Varginha ET and Railway Line." From there, negotiations and ethnographic studies began to identify how the Varginha ET mobilizes the local population's imagination, with a view to possible registration.
--- seg-90b762f83fd8d240aea9fe81 [needs-review] ---

--- seg-4cfbcd5c2dd9e16df5cb6b11 [needs-review] ---
In 2021, the educational action "Playing with Heritage" was carried out, which integrates the project "Our Heritage, Our Treasure". The project is a proposal from the Technical Coordination of Cultural Heritage and the Municipal Public Library aimed at elementary school education. The action included a virtual lecture and video presentation that discusses the history of the city, historical, cultural, and artistic heritage, and its main concepts in an interactive way, with the participation of 5th year B students from the SESI Varginha - Unit II elementary school. The goal was to introduce and awaken interest in these students about their heritage.

===== PAGE 189 [page-8b86470ef648b30894037b39] =====
--- seg-182f49e528f4b78a8d489cd9 [needs-review] ---
Registration Process for intangible property: "The E.T. Case and the Varginha Identity"

Municipality of Varginha | MG

Year 2023 | Exercise 2025
--- seg-8dc843055d95c8b3bbe00e3a [needs-review] ---
Students were introduced to the knowledge, awareness, and need to value and preserve the cultural heritage of Varginha through a lecture on heritage, the history of the municipality, and key concepts related to the topic, such as culture, memory, designation, heritage, and cultural assets. A digital folder titled "Our Heritage, Our Treasure" was provided, including a map and a brief history of the protected heritage sites in the city for student and teacher reference.

To narrate the history of the municipality, lecturer and teacher Eliana Cristina Costa showed students a video about the Nina doll in Varginha. Thus, the lecture presented to students the definitions of memory, culture, identity, designation, and the importance and influence of cultural assets on the development of the municipality, memory preservation, traditions, and social and political transformations in the city. A brief history of some of the main material assets in Varginha, such as the Railway Station, Afonso Pena State School, Dom Pedro II Square, City Hall, and the Old Forum, was also selected and presented to the students. Activities titled "What I Would Like to Become Cultural Heritage" and "Drawing Varginha's Cultural Heritage" were conducted.

During the presentation of the city's history, it was observed that the children were visibly surprised to learn the true facts about Varginha's history, as the incident that occurred in 1996, involving a supposed appearance of an extraterrestrial on municipal territory, had become so ingrained in Varginha's culture that younger generations know little or nothing about the historical events preceding the arrival of this remarkable folk figure and legend to the municipality.

Regarding the pedagogical activities of the workshop, two freehand drawing activities were carried out: "What I Would Like to Become Cultural Heritage" and another titled "Drawing Varginha's Cultural Heritage." In the first activity, students, through free drawing, selected new cultural heritage sites. When analyzing the students' drawings, it was observed that the "ET's Ship" holds significant cultural value for the students. In the second activity, students drew some local cultural heritage sites in the municipality. The activities were adapted due to the COVID-19 pandemic.
--- seg-81a20fcbe8ccb955c3a9b75d [needs-review] ---

--- seg-25f9d008df281702ff59ea9b [needs-review] ---
"Luis the mute": the creature sighted in the interior of Minas Gerais, Brazil, in the city of Varginha on January 20, 1996. Several witnesses claimed to have seen the creature, or creatures. Around this date, several military operations took place in the city, in addition to rumors of strange things happening there. It is believed that the creatures were captured by the army and are still in their possession. The case became known as the Varginha ET case.

29) Zedi, mascot of the Shopping Via Café Garden in Varginha
--- seg-b3ed9244a21f00b53edd83c7 [needs-review] ---
At the Via Café Garden Shopping, considered one of the largest shopping centers in southern Minas Gerais, located in Varginha, visitors are greeted by a smiling sculpture representing the Varginha Alien. It's common to see children hugging the sculpture of the most famous character from Varginha.

Zedi, the name given to this version of the Alien, is the shopping center's mascot. The choice was made through a popular vote in 2015. With 70% of the votes, Zedi, an intelligent and modern E.T., was elected. The other mascot, called "Dedeco," a coffee bag companion who loves to be with friends and highlights the importance of the coffee-producing region, lost the vote. Although the shopping center's name refers to coffee, the public chose a friendly little alien as their mascot.
--- seg-8b2ffd44bf9f4c981afe272c [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | Mayor: Verdi Lúcio Melo | Sector Head: Danielle de Souza Guimarães

--- seg-3689b5b43922799d6b7579a8 [needs-review] ---
In his travels around the world seeking knowledge and business contacts, Henrique was always asked about the curious story of his hometown, the so-called "ET city." The topic was so prevalent that it sparked hours of conversations and debates about its peculiarities and truthfulness. Not long after, friends in the business occasionally started calling him an ET.

On a cold night in 2006, while working on the inscriptions on the front and rear panels of a new product, and chatting about audio in a social network group, the idea of putting an ET design on the equipment arose. At first, it seemed like a joke, but the concept was immediately implemented in a relaxed manner, placing a Brazilian flag, a Minas Gerais flag, and another with an alien figure above the "made in brazil" inscription. The subtle symbolism, displayed in the social network group, received criticism but was acclaimed by a wide majority and became a presence in all products manufactured by the brand since then, eventually becoming a company icon.

The year 2012 was marked by significant changes. The sharp growth of the company required the adoption of new management tools, larger production space, a team of even more prepared collaborators, and much more innovative products. Our renowned line of energy managers was undergoing a deep overhaul, and on the eve of its launch, while reviewing a supplier's catalog, we noticed that the buttons on its keys could serve as the eyes of an ET. We changed the design, and from there, our current alien mascot symbol was born. It still adorns the front panel of this equipment line today.

36) Main changes related to the good
--- seg-5dcf8a9dc1d93ec8183e8eaf [needs-review] ---
One of the main changes that occurred over time was how the Varginha ET was represented and its many versions. The story, what we call the generating fact, never changed, but the representations did. Initially, the ET was depicted in brown with features similar to those described by the girls who reportedly saw the creature. The earliest sculptures by Mauri Bomfim, for example, from 1996, are more faithful to the "spoken portrait" of the girls who saw the creature, as seen in the photograph of these first pieces. Later, other more playful and "friendly" versions were created, and the ET began to be represented in green as well. The reason for this change, according to those involved in creating the Varginha ET versions, is that the Varginha ET images should appeal to and not repel children. The more playful versions with a smiling ET or with stereotypical rural stereotypes, such as football players, with coffee, among others, attract more than the other versions, which are the most faithful to the testimonies of the girls who reportedly saw the creature. However, the two versions, or better yet, the many versions and aesthetics of the Varginha ET coexist.
--- seg-5c1607cc16a89edca54e42b7 [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | Mayor: Verdi Lúcio Melo | Sector Head: Danielle de Souza Guimarães
                     Responsible Sector: Cultural Heritage Technical Coordination
              Praça Matheus Tavares, 121 | Neighborhood: Center | Zip Code: 37002-320 | Tel.: (35) 3690-2718
--- seg-42607f9c76e9b92fbef42e5a [machine-unreviewed] ---

37) Possibilities of continuation
--- seg-b5d243d091423f9dfd5c02da [needs-review] ---
The proposed Safeguarding Plan outlines actions that ensure the preservation of the asset in the present and its continuity into the future. During the investigations, it was identified that the asset is alive in local, national, and international memory and is not currently at risk of disappearance. The continuity of the asset is achieved through the natural process of diffusion carried out by the press, ufologists, and all those who have created and continue to create versions of the Varginha ET character.

4. Justifications for Registration
--- seg-fa641cfe694dbdfae01a2e4a [needs-review] ---
The request for the registration of the Varginha ET case as local heritage originated from the Varginha community itself, which considers it a unique cultural asset of the municipality. Therefore, there was a movement by citizens to identify and declare this asset as local intangible heritage.

The Varginha ET case has been perpetuated in the collective memory of the local, national, and international communities since 1996 and is constantly portrayed and recreated through the media, films, audiovisual records, books, crafts, cartoons, games, patrimonial education, sculptures, and other aspects described in this Dossier. Moreover, the asset is reflected in the architecture and furniture related to the Varginha ET case in the urban center of the municipality.

Initially, the incident gained widespread coverage through the press and ufologists, but later it transcended these boundaries and became used by various other individuals within the municipality, across Brazil, and even worldwide, as evidenced by the recent documentary released in the United States and produced by James Fox, "Moment of Contact" (2022). Another recent documentary was produced by Japanese TV and TV Tokyo, set for release. Both documentaries were filmed in 2022 and released between 2022 and 2023.

The Varginha ET case is considered by the global ufology community as the second most important case in the world of a supposed extraterrestrial appearance. The first case is that of Roswell, which occurred in July 1947 in the city of the same name in New Mexico, USA, involving the supposed appearance of an unidentified flying object.

It is important to emphasize that the speech position for this case is the Varginha community itself, which recognizes the asset as local cultural heritage, as evidenced by the expressions in favor of registration through signatures on consents and petitions attached to this Dossier. The hundreds of representations created and recreated daily, which keep the memory of the asset alive, also reinforce its local recognition as a cultural asset.

The protection sought through this registration, as strictly described in this Dossier, concerns the representations created within the municipality regarding the Varginha ET case. The meaning
--- seg-9213a082646da4e3b762a3ff [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | Mayor: Verdi Lúcio Melo | Sector Head: Danielle de Souza Guimarães
                     Responsible Sector: Cultural Heritage Technical Coordination
              Praça Matheus Tavares, 121 | Neighborhood: Center | Zip Code: 37002-320 | Tel.: (35) 3690-2718
--- seg-dd2485efa9fd8eadb6c72921 [machine-unreviewed] ---
Page 222 of 403

===== PAGE 223 [page-4f95f3c6a25a9b35d59f8305] =====
--- seg-c8148f4b85ddffbd4c5d6fa5 [needs-review] ---
Registration Process for intangible property: "The E.T. Case and the Varginha Identity"

Municipality of Varginha | MG

Year 2023 | Exercise 2025
--- seg-acdbe2b608fc230281ee2e3e [needs-review] ---
The representation presented in this Registration Dossier is based on the definitions by Carlo Ginsburg and Roger Chartier, aligning with UNESCO's understanding that representations, alongside practices, expressions, knowledge, and techniques, can be recognized as Intangible Cultural Heritage. To this end, a new municipal Registration Book, the Book of Registration of Representations, was opened to inscribe representations, such as the Varginha ET. The asset is also being inscribed in the Book of Places.

Moreover, one of the possibilities for recognizing an asset of Intangible and Material Cultural Heritage is through associations and representations made about it over time through music, poetry, literature, images, souvenirs, cartoons, statues, monuments, buildings, paintings, magazine and newspaper publications, online and offline, among others. The Varginha ET case, as demonstrated in this study, has hundreds of recreations, representations, and versions of the Varginha ET persona since 1996 to the present day. This keeps the asset constantly alive in collective imagination and offers possibilities for recreation and continuity.

For UNESCO, the associated values present in the representations project the culture and significant aspects of the place represented. When representations of what would be extraterrestrials appear in animations, cartoons, or books drinking coffee and eating cheese bread, it refers to the local Varginha and Minas Gerais culture as a whole. These representations, indeed, make Varginha recognizable through them. If a person is photographed under the replica of the spacecraft installed by COPASA in Varginha's center, it's not necessary for the person to describe their location to recognize that they are in Varginha. In other words, the representation, in this case, the mockup of the spacecraft, takes the place of the represented thing, which in this case is Varginha and the relationship with the history of the ET and UFOs.

Varginha became known worldwide as the city of the Varginha ET from what became known as the "Varginha Incident" or "Varginha Incident" or, alternatively, "Case of the Varginha ET." This is an undeniable fact and is not a point of discussion. For these reasons, the asset is also being inscribed in the Book of Places, due to the understanding that the entire territory of Varginha is identified as "ET's land."

With the disclosure and great repercussion of the case, the municipality of Varginha "gained" an E.T., as in all references about the alien, it appears as Varginha's E.T.
--- seg-78bf36e1933fe7d80f853158 [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | Mayor: Verdi Lúcio Melo | Sector Head: Danielle de Souza Guimarães
                     Responsible Sector: Cultural Heritage Technical Coordination
              Praça Matheus Tavares, 121 | Neighborhood: Center | Zip Code: 37002-320 | Tel.: (35) 3690-2718

Municipality of Varginha | MG

--- seg-d37fe2697a80aaed3b6d584b [needs-review] ---
It is an ET in Varginha, or an ET that was in Varginha. But it is "of," a personal pronoun.
The ET is recognized as being from Varginha, even charges and playful videos refer to an ET who became a miner, wears boots, enjoys cheese bread, and talks with the distinctive accent of the people from the interior of Minas Gerais. Representations of the ET who is said to be "of" Varginha appear stereotyped with signs that refer to the people of the interior of Minas Gerais, whether by accent, clothing, and taste for coffee and cheese bread.

The consistency of representations, discourses, and images created and recreated around the ET case in Varginha has kept the memory of the event alive, even for those who have never been to Varginha. In the national and global imagination, Varginha is the municipality where the strange creatures allegedly appeared in January 1996, identified by ufologists as extraterrestrial beings. In this sense, the ET is from Varginha, it is in Varginha, even if the supposed appearance event only happened once. It's as if the territory of Varginha is the territory of ETs, or at least those who allegedly appeared there. It is very likely that the mystery and secrecy imposed by the authorities, especially the military, around the case fuels imagination, and new representations and narratives are created over time.

We are referring to a community that was culturally marked by a particular event, in this case, the ET incident in Varginha. When Varginha is mentioned, whether in Brazilian territory or elsewhere, the first association made is to the ET case, as amply demonstrated in this Dossier. Although coffee is the most prominent and enduring product that previously represented the municipality, currently, the ET case projects Varginha onto Brazil and the world.

Registration as local intangible heritage will enable safeguarding actions focused on patrimonial education directed towards astronomy studies, spatial knowledge, and ufology. However, it is not about proving or asserting the existence or non-existence of extraterrestrial beings and their spacecraft. Rather, it is about recognizing a collective memory that created representations and how these representations forged a local identity, enabling hundreds of recreations and the continuity of the good. The good has persisted in the local, national, and global imagination without any impetus from the public power.
