Varginha. The idea is that now the public power should be part of this diffusion, impulsion,
and safeguarding actions.
        It is important to highlight that, as a cultural fact, the Varginha ET Case was forged
by the Varginha community itself, by ufologists, and by those who appropriated some good in
order to create versions and representations that have been perpetuated in national and world
memory. We can surely affirm that the Varginha ET Case and all the representations, narratives,
mysteries, and mythifications it sparked, have made it a good far beyond the territorial limits
of Varginha. The Varginha ET Case and everything surrounding it has a national reach.
        The current mayor, Vérdi Melo, stated regarding the Registry that "The ET Case made
our city world-known. Today, we receive visitors from all over, scholars of the case spread the
story of Varginha, and it is up to us, public managers, to take care of this illustrious visitor
who chose our city to land."
        For José Manoel Magalhães Ferreira, president of the Municipal Cultural Heritage

indelebilly marked the identity of the municipality and the way it is seen and represented.
Even children demonstrate this understanding through drawings, as shown in the Education
Heritage section.

representative, symbolic, identity, and cultural values, the cultural good "The ET Case and
Varginha's Identity" was indicated for studies and Registered as Intangible Cultural Heritage
of Varginha.

6. Opening a new register book - Book of Representations
--- seg-b55e8af61a869e7e4dd0955c [needs-review] ---
According to the decision of the Municipal Cultural Heritage Council of Varginha, at its 218th meeting, held on May 18, 2023, the Council decided to open a new Register Book for the inscription of Varginha's intangible cultural heritage. The Register Book of Representations was created in accordance with Article 2, § 2 of Municipal Decree No. 8.818/18.

After several discussions regarding the nature of the asset "The E.T. Case and Varginha's Identity," it was concluded that a new Register Book was necessary due to the diffuse nature of the asset and the characteristics of this Register, where the asset is dispersed among distinct representations based on narratives and ideas. The asset is not a cultural practice, a form of expression, a technique, knowledge, or celebration. Its appropriation occurs in a diffuse manner, unlike other intangible cultural heritage assets that typically develop and persist within specific social groups and contexts.

UNESCO defines intangible cultural heritage as "the practices, representations, expressions, knowledge, and techniques—together with the instruments, objects, artifacts, and cultural spaces associated with them—that communities, groups, and in some cases individuals recognize as an integral part of their cultural heritage." This definition is outlined in Article 2 of the Convention for the Safeguarding of Intangible Cultural Heritage.

The term "representation" derives from the Latin term 'repraesentare', meaning to make present or to present again.

According to some authors, such as Francisco Falcon (1985), the term representation, within the concepts of social sciences or history, lies at the center of a constellation of diverse notions or concepts, including imagination(s), ideology(s), myth(s), and mythology(s), utopia(s), and memory(s).

The concept of representation presented here follows the definitions and concepts of Carlo Ginzburg in his book *Wooden Eyes*, and Roger Chartier's study *The World as Representation*, which is included in the book *On the Edge of the Cliff*.
--- seg-703f004aaefd117176713e8e [needs-review] ---

                     Responsible Sector: Cultural Heritage Technical Coordination
              Praça Matheus Tavares, 121 | Neighborhood: Center | Zip Code: 37002-320 | Tel.: (35) 3690-2718

===== PAGE 232 [page-154fbe4d1b22111321cc7a4d] =====
--- seg-9d06505420fc5664ddaf1e97 [needs-review] ---
Registration Process for intangible property: "The E.T. Case and the Varginha Identity"

Municipality of Varginha | MG

Year 2023 | Fiscal Year 2025
--- seg-ec0c3c50bca204791ead370a [needs-review] ---
Ginzburg argues that, on one hand, "representation" stands in for the reality it represents, thus evoking absence. On the other hand, it makes the represented reality visible, thus suggesting presence. He calls this "a boring game of mirrors," as the opposition could easily be reversed: in the first case, representation is present, albeit as a substitute; in the second, it ultimately refers, by contrast, to the absent reality it aims to represent (GINZBURG, p. 85). Absence and presence are always a duality, a pair, in representations.

Ginzburg believes that an image is both presence and a substitute for something absent. In certain contexts, representation takes the place of the thing represented. In the case of the Varginha ET, regardless of whether it actually existed or not, what we always have is the representation of the event. Not the event itself, which is past, that existed at a specific moment and was experienced by a limited group of people. The question of representation in this case serves for a historical fact, something verifiable and feasible, but also for a work of fiction, myths, beliefs, among others. For example, was the Yeti (considered the abominable snowman) real or a product of human imagination? Whether it actually existed or not, it never prevented various representations, narratives, and even concrete searches from occurring to locate the mysterious mountain monster. In this case, as with the Varginha ET, representations took the place of the thing represented and took on a much greater proportion than the Yeti itself, regardless of whether it existed or not.

For Chartier, the representation relationship should be understood as a relationship between a present image and an absent object, one standing in for the other because it is its homolog. Once again, the issue of presence/absence is at stake. Both Chartier and Pierre Bourdier, a French sociologist, understand that representations are social constructions of historical experiences. These two theorists, Chartier and Bourdier, revisit the discussion of representation initiated by Marcel Mauss and Émile Durkheim.

It is in the sense of representation as something that replaces the represented thing and creates new representations, versions, and meanings that we understand the opening of this new Record Book.

Chartier argues that the concept of representation is also central to the construction of social identities. Drawing on Ginzburg, Chartier wrote that in the formation of these identities, representation plays a crucial role in shaping collective memories and narratives.

--- seg-2b324d8a18e340fee3f94455 [needs-review] ---

--- seg-4d8f342d8028bb9a969c9512 [needs-review] ---
Social identities, there is always a power dynamic between the representations imposed by those who hold the power to classify and name, and, on the other side, the definition, acceptance, or resistance that each community produces about itself. In this power relationship, the representations of the Varginha ET Case emerged and persisted in the collective imagination, even with the denial of authorities such as the Army, Fire Department, Military Police, and also local government, which only in the 2000s recognized and highlighted the Varginha ET story. Despite jokes and public incredulity, the event endured because a community, local or foreign, produced narratives and representations that resonated with the case and generated new narratives to keep it alive in collective memory.

Mário Ribeiro Duarte (2009) wrote that "the Varginha ET can be understood as the creation of a local tradition, configured from elements derived from literature, cinema, and the internet, ultimately from a distant culture that was incorporated/adapted to the local." For him, the space became global, we are all integrated, connected, and therefore, we participate, influence, and are influenced by what happens from the frozen steppes of Siberian Russia, through Alaska, New Mexico, Machu Picchu, to Varginha (DUARTE, 2009).

Duarte (2009) also states that the "Varginha ET" created a local market, while simultaneously inserting the city into a much larger market than what existed before. Varginha was publicized and recognized worldwide as the city of the ET. Thus, creating a local identification using symbolism from global imagination. Here, once again, Varginha is taken as "the city of the ET," a personal pronoun. The ET is from Varginha because Varginha is the city of the ET. It is from Varginha, from the territory of the municipality in southern Minas Gerais, and nowhere else.

Given the multiplicity of representations of this memory and collective experiences around the Varginha ET case, as well as the completely diffuse nature of its materialization, we identify that the currently existing Register Books are incapable of encompassing the nuances of this new category of intangible cultural asset that proposes itself.

Therefore, to avoid creating a new Register Book exclusively for the inscription of the asset that led to its conceptualization and origin, we suggest that it serve to register assets related to imagination, memory, ideologies, legends,

7. Community consent and involvement and interviews
--- seg-532f3f5057b7a95c2351d9ec [needs-review] ---
Due to the diffuse nature of the good, it is complex to determine who the holders would be. A priori, every resident of Varginha would be a holder of the cultural good, as they reside in the municipality. However, it is possible to identify groups with stronger connections to the good, such as artisans gathered in local associations: the Minas Artisan Association (AMINARTE); the Association of Popular Artisans and Artists of Varginha - ASSOART; the Association of Varginha Retired and Pensioners - AAPV; and the Varginha Creative Economy Association - AVEC. As well as cartoonists, illustrators, designers, artists, merchants, musicians, representatives of entities like the Gerais Paths Circuit, journalists, documentarians, teachers, architects, ufologists, among others.

In addition to these directly related to the creation of representations and the preservation of living memory and the possibilities of the good's continuity, the local government, represented by the Mayor, Secretaries, and their staff, are also important holders who will enable the implementation of the good's Safeguarding.

Throughout the Registration process, numerous individuals were interviewed and directly involved in the studies, providing accounts, indicating other people, making descriptions, and demonstrating how the good has remained latent over time.

Below, we include the signed consents and, likewise, the signature lists from the petitions collected throughout the municipality.

===== PAGE 341 [page-ec8d68d01b55599a8a882ea3] =====
--- seg-b7348632e991e3791b6c4d2a [needs-review] ---
Registration Process for intangible property: "The E.T. Case and the Varginha Identity"

Municipality of Varginha | MG

Year 2023 | Fiscal Year 2025
--- seg-95a58b2048c514c0509060f0 [needs-review] ---
25 years ago, Brazil was introduced to the mysterious case of the Varginha ET
BY HISTORY CHANNEL BRAZIL ON JANUARY 20, 2021 AT 02:52 PM
--- seg-f7855fc21298d5999ae84c86 [needs-review] ---
Exactly 25 years ago, on January 20, 1996, Brazil was surprised by the Varginha ET case, an incident that allegedly occurred in the interior of Minas Gerais. Everything started when three girls from that city claimed to have seen a creature with brown, viscous skin, large red eyes, and a bulky head with protuberances. Other witnesses at the time also reported seeing a UFO in the area.
--- seg-1b765bfdf7de6ffebb855473 [needs-review] ---
According to rumors, an UFO allegedly crashed at the site and a dead alien was taken to Campinas for an autopsy. The local Fire Department and Military Police denied all information. To this day, there is no evidence that extraterrestrial beings have visited the Minas Gerais interior. Nevertheless, the story became an urban legend, and the Varginha ET entered national folklore.
--- seg-1e4cf78db59e9a18c25452d2 [needs-review] ---
But what was the true origin of the appearance? According to a 1997 investigation, a great misunderstanding had given rise to the Varginha ET. The girls, in reality, had encountered a citizen known as Mudinho, who lived with his family in front of the property where the alleged sighting took place. This man, who suffered from mental issues, had the habit of crouching down to pick up small objects from the ground.
--- seg-6a955aaf226b4856a8e65c03 [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | Mayor: Verdi Lúcio Melo | Sector Head: Danielle de Souza Guimarães
                          Responsible Sector: Cultural Heritage Technical Coordination
                 Praça Matheus Tavares, 121 | Neighborhood: Center | Zip Code: 37002-320 | Tel.: (35) 3690-2718
--- seg-ac70bdf85ebcd87593b5a170 [machine-unreviewed] ---
Page 341 of 403

===== PAGE 342 [page-e2d3a8efd575713184ebe382] =====
--- seg-69c23da7b36bb1cf87289a20 [needs-review] ---
Registration Process for intangible property: "The E.T. Case and the Varginha Identity"

Municipality of Varginha | MG

Year 2023 | Fiscal Year 2025
--- seg-1f714df17afd47f115f93d26 [machine-unreviewed] ---
On the day of the incident, Mudinho might have been covered in mud due to the heavy rains and scared the girls
who mistook him for an alien creature.
--- seg-0064a1376c841bc797e5645d [machine-unreviewed] ---
Although alien presence has been ruled out, the city of Varginha attracts many tourists due to the story. In fact, one of the city's tourist attractions is a large water tank in the center, in the shape of a spaceship.

Varginha ET: The case turns 20 years old with mysteries and uncertainties
Three girls reportedly saw an extraterrestrial in January 1996. The city became worldwide known
for the case, which was never clarified.
Varginha, January 20, 1996. It would have been a normal day in the quiet town of 100,000 inhabitants in
Southern Minas, but the account of three girls changed everything. They said they saw a strange, short
creature with red eyes, who didn't seem of this world. Overnight, researchers and numerous witnesses
began talking about the "Varginha ET".
More reports emerged about unidentified creatures and objects, military operations, strange movements
in the city, and mysterious deaths. The case was featured in ufology publications around the world. In
these 20 years, many doubts remained in the air. But it's certain that the story is part of Brazilian
imagination and terrified many people. Remind the main

In the 20th anniversary of the Varginha ET, artists present new images
The supposed extraterrestrial appearance marks two decades of mystery. To celebrate the anniversary, artists
offer their versions of the ET.
The supposed Varginha ET appearance, which turns 20 this Wednesday (20), captivates the imagination of many.
In the days following the reports of the three girls who allegedly saw the creature in an empty lot, many artists
recreated images of what the supposed extraterrestrial might look like based on their descriptions.
To commemorate two decades of mystery surrounding the Varginha ET, G1 asked new artists to draw or create
cartoons of what they imagined the supposed ET to be.
"It's fun to work with a story that's part of the Brazilian imagination, to play with a case that has raised so many
questions for two decades. Even more so for someone like me, who grew up in Pouso Alegre, in the same region as
Varginha. I hope internet users enjoy the cartoon as much as I did creating it," said journalist Jarbas Soares,
who believes that after so much interaction with humans, the ET might even enjoy pão de queijo (a traditional
Brazilian cheese bread).

The Varginha Case, an ufological incident pointed as the most notable of its kind in Brazil throughout history, marks 15 years in January. Located in southern Minas Gerais, Varginha entered the global ufology map after three girls reported facing an extraterrestrial being. The fascination of the story, beyond this third-degree direct contact, was tied to a fact defended by the ufological community, which sponsored a pilgrimage to the Minas Gerais city. According to them, the Brazilian Army, the Military Police, and the Fire Department captured on January 20, 1996, two creatures from another planet and took them for analyses in hospitals and a necropsy in Campinas, São Paulo. These institutions, according to ufologists, have been withholding the disclosure of these facts since then.

Varginha City Hall inaugurates E.T. Memorial
--- seg-5b17ae5ac3c8270fd85904fa [needs-review] ---
Space is already a business card and also features a planetarium that will show educational films.
The Varginha City Hall is delivering to the population this week one of the largest tourist attractions, the
ET Memorial, along with the Planetarium, located on the top of Vila Paiva, where the Municipal Secretariat of Tourism and Commerce operates.
The ET Memorial is named after "Radialista Galvão Conde", a deceased journalist in 2017 at the age of 84, who dedicated his life to communication and worked to promote Varginha's name through radio waves.
The Planetarium will show short 360° educational and cultural films.
With an inauguration scheduled for this Tuesday, October 8, at 7 p.m., these spaces promise to be attractions due to the great success of the VARGINHA ET case, which will have its story told through suspended panels and videos on nine televisions installed on playful ET hands, created by artist Gleiber Piva.
The City Hall states that it is a topic that touches the imagination of many people and invites scholars, researchers, and ufologists to join this special visit to Varginha, which became internationally known for the UFO incident.
The event will feature the presence of ufologists Vitório Pacaccini, Marco Aurelio, Jamil Villanova, and others of renown in international ufology.
The young women who witnessed the creatures in the afternoon of January 20, 1996, were also invited.
ET Memorial
The ET Memorial project was announced in 2008, with the work remaining stalled for years. In 2013, when Antônio Silva and Vérdi Melo took office, they committed to completing the work.
Now, under the Vérdi administration, the monument is being delivered to the public.

The ET Memorial will be inaugurated on the evening of this Tuesday (8), in the Vila Paiva neighborhood, in Varginha (MG). The project was announced in 2008, was temporarily halted, and now, after 14 years, it will be delivered to the public.

In addition to being a tourist attraction, the site will serve an educational purpose with a planetarium, showing educational films and the history of the Varginha ET case. The story will be told through suspended panels and videos on nine televisions installed in playful ET hands, created by artist Gleiber Piva.

The event will include the presence of the three girls, now women, who witnessed the creatures on January 20, 1996, as well as ufologists Vitório Pacaccini, Marco Aurélio, Jamil Villanova, and others of international renown.

===== PAGE 357 [page-4bcfe1bff2df4f7c474ddcf8] =====
--- seg-da884b36d5ee573fa8b5dd6f [needs-review] ---
Registration Process for intangible property: "The E.T. Case and the Varginha Identity"

Municipality of Varginha | MG

Year 2023 | Exercise 2025
--- seg-91a8786e8e46367ff36eff53 [needs-review] ---
The Varginha City Hall is conducting studies to register the "Varginha ET Case" as an Intangible Cultural Heritage Dossier. In March 2023, Varginha's Cultural Heritage Council accepted requests from the local community to initiate studies for a municipal registration dossier regarding the "Varginha ET Case."

The "Varginha Case," referring to the events in January 1996 involving unidentified flying object (UFO) sightings and mysterious non-human entities, made Varginha renowned worldwide as the "ET City." Since then, based on reports from local residents and ufological studies widely disseminated by national and international media, Varginha has organized itself over the past 20 years, shaping its narrative as the "ET City."

Various efforts have been made to construct this image, including the projection of architectural and urban furniture that directly allude to the representation of what is imagined to be an extraterrestrial creature and UFOs, commonly known as "flying saucers."

Recently, American filmmaker James Fox released the documentary "Moment of Contact" (or "The Varginha Case: Moment of Contact"), which demonstrates that the story remains vibrant, fostering discussions, new reports, and interest even to this day, preserving the memory of the 1996 events.

In this context of the strong identity created in Varginha Municipality related to the ET and UFO narrative, the registration process is situated.

Today, we receive visitors from all over, scholars of the case spread the Varginha incident, and it is up to us, public managers, to take care of this illustrious visitor who chose our city to land." said Mayor Vérdi Melo.

--- seg-d4171296ac44dac5473a590d [needs-review] ---
Brazil Beyond: 'abducted' in Varginha, Boa Esporte gets a new name and ET
--- seg-412b357d58d5b3ff2da6d4d4 [needs-review] ---
Contested among municipalities, the former Ituiutaba changes its address and adopts the symbol of the new city to increase its identification with the local public. By Thiago de Lima, Rio de Janeiro
--- seg-8092f4777162ee0b2fcbcce2 [needs-review] ---
On January 20, 1996. The story in Varginha, a city in southern Minas Gerais, goes that on this date, following
the appearances of UFOs (Unidentified Flying Objects) flying over the region, three teenagers allegedly encountered
an extraterrestrial being, which was later captured and kept under secrecy by the Brazilian Army. The case made
its way around the world and became the most famous alien phenomenon in the country. Fifteen years later, like
an UFO, the Boa Esporte Clube landed in the city under the name of another Minas Gerais municipality, Ituiutaba,
without identification with the local population. But to gain the sympathy of the residents, in addition to changing
how it was called, the team adopted a new mascot: the Varginha ET.
--- seg-c46ad27e4114f8670e5eedfa [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | Mayor: Verdi Lúcio Melo | Sector Head: Danielle de Souza Guimarães

                 Praça Matheus Tavares, 121 | Neighborhood: Center | Zip Code: 37002-320 | Tel.: (35) 3690-2718

===== PAGE 362 [page-b8089f1474ceba620f77ef4d] =====

Year 2023 | Fiscal Year 2025
--- seg-2212889c59a7f350bce907a9 [needs-review] ---
Next to the owl, the official symbol of the club, the extraterrestrial enters the field in every Boa Esporte game at the Prefeito Dilzon Luiz de Melo Stadium, popularly known as Melão, in Varginha. With a capacity of 15,400 people, the stadium was the first reason for the many changes that were to come. Still named Ituiutaba Esporte Clube, the team played its matches at the Fazendinha Stadium in Ituiutaba, which currently holds around 1,800 people. But after finishing as vice-champion of last year's Série C and qualifying for the national second division, which requires a minimum of 15,000 seats in stadiums, the team found itself without a place to host its games, a fact that sparked Varginha's interest.

10. Safeguarding and Diagnosis Plan for Cultural Heritage at the time of Registration
--- seg-2878ecef734151f55de1a137 [needs-review] ---
The immaterial good identified as “The Case of E.T. and the Varginha Identity” is alive and remains active in the representations of the municipality of Varginha. Research for the elaboration of the Dossier identified that the story of the alleged appearance of extraterrestrial creatures continues to be alive, fostering discussions, new reports, and interest until the present day. All these factors ensure that the memory of the events that occurred in 1996 remains alive and allows for new recreations and the continuity of the good.

However, it is not the fact itself, known as the Varginha E.T. Case, that we want to protect and safeguard, but the representations and narratives that were made around the story and that projected Varginha onto the global ufology scene. These representations created an identity for the municipality of Varginha, which "gained" an E.T., as well as narratives and discourses.

We identified that there was first an outward movement regarding the Varginha E.T. Case, and only later did the municipality itself recognize that narrative. Some citizens, like the artisan Mauri Bomfim, made the first representations in large statues and sculptures immediately after the event, still in 1996. After him, dozens of people followed, creating diverse representations present in crafts, buildings, urban furniture, sculptures, cartoons, drawings, among others.

The persistence of the Varginha E.T. Case in the media, in monuments and buildings, in cartoons, in crafts, among others, kept a collective memory alive. This memory projected Varginha beyond its borders, with the image of the municipality linked to the idea of the E.T.

The goal of the Registration and the construction of a Safeguarding Plan is to allow astronomy to be explored alongside spatial and universe knowledge, encouraging and fostering Patrimonial Education. Similarly, these actions aim to encourage ufology studies in the municipality. The Archive of the Cultural Foundation of Varginha also intends to create a fund on the Varginha E.T. Case and all the representations made about it.

These actions will further promote the theme, benefiting the holders, scattered among artisans, merchants, among others.
--- seg-48f0e6a116641aa0be052649 [needs-review] ---

10.1          Graphical schedule of safeguard actions
--- seg-e3372b15752c41b0e03e6cba [needs-review] ---
Legend:

Em 1994, um piloto comercial relatou ter visto um objeto em forma de disco voador (OVNI) sobre o estado do Texas, EUA. O objeto era de cor prateada e se movia lentamente no céu noturno. O piloto ficou tão impressionado que decidiu registrar o incidente.

Ele descreveu o objeto como tendo cerca de 50 metros de diâmetro e emitindo uma luz fraca e azulada. O piloto relatou que o OVNI manobrou em um padrão circular antes de desaparecer repentinamente.

Este relato é apenas um dos muitos casos de avistamentos de OVNIs no Texas. Ao longo dos anos, o estado tem sido palco de inúmeras observações de objetos voadores não identificados, deixando muitos moradores e especialistas intrigados.

Os céticos argumentam que esses avistamentos podem ser explicados por fenômenos naturais, aeronaves convencionais ou até mesmo ilusões de ótica. No entanto, para muitos, a presença de OVNIs no Texas continua a ser um mistério intrigante.
